# GasGas MC 125 2021: aftermarket parts

4533 listings whose stated fitment names this bike: 324 researched parts (with what they change and rider findings), 684 dealer and retailer listings specific to this bike, and 3525 universal retailer listings (oil, grips, batteries and the like, counted below; every listing is in [parts.csv](../parts.csv)). Research candidates: check fitment on the maker's page before relying on a row. Back to [the family catalog](../CATALOG.md).
